Google 发布 Gemini 3.6 Flash、3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash Cyber

Introducing Gemini 3.6 Flash, 3.5 Flash-Lite, and 3.5 Flash Cyber

Google DeepMind 推出新一批 Gemini 模型，包括 Gemini 3.6 Flash、3.5 Flash-Lite 以及面向安全场景的 3.5 Flash Cyber。

英文原文 EN

We’re introducing new Gemini models, including Gemini 3.6 Flash, 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash Cyber.

洞察Google 把主力节奏从旗舰 Pro 切到 Flash 家族，说明大厂已经把利润焦点从『谁最强』移到『谁能便宜到嵌入每一次请求』；Cyber 变体的出现意味着行业级 SLM 会成为未来一年新的岗位方向，尤其是安全 + 模型融合的横向人才。
招聘行动把 Gemini Flash / Flash-Lite / Cyber 团队(含 pre-training、post-training、safety-tuning、serving infra)拆成独立 mapping cluster，重点跟进有小模型蒸馏与低延迟推理经验的核心成员；同时把候选人评估维度加入『能否为特定垂直行业做安全对齐』这一项。
Google DeepMind Blog · Tue, 21 Ju 原文 ↗
★ 本周重点Frontier RadarOpenAI技术突破官方频道安全对齐

长程模型(long-horizon models)时代下的安全与对齐

Safety and alignment in an era of long-horizon models

OpenAI 分享部署长时运行 AI 模型的经验教训，重点阐述新出现的安全风险、观测到的失败案例，以及通过迭代部署强化的安全保障措施。

英文原文 EN

OpenAI shares lessons from deploying long-running AI models, highlighting new safety risks, observed failures, and improved safeguards through iterative deployment.

洞察这条公告是行业信号弹：当模型开始跨小时/跨天执行任务时，评测、对齐与红队（red team）能力不再是可选项。OpenAI 把 Safety Systems、Preparedness、Alignment 三条线合流的痕迹越来越明显，也意味着 Anthropic 的 RSP、DeepMind 的 Frontier Safety Framework 都会加速升级。
招聘行动招聘/mapping 侧应立刻更新 Safety & Alignment 岗位画像：不再只是 RLHF/red team，而是『长时程 Agent 评测 + 可解释性 + 工具使用治理』复合背景；重点 mapping 对象包括 OpenAI Safety Systems、Alignment Team、Anthropic Alignment Science、DeepMind AGI Safety，并挖掘从这些组织流出的 Founding Engineer 级候选人。

★ 本周重点Frontier Radar社区/其他观点访谈深度长访谈大模型架构训练方法开源生态算力基建

走进模型工厂——Poolside AI 联合 CEO Eiso Kant

Inside the Model Factory — Eiso Kant, Poolside AI

Latent Space 对话 Poolside 联合 CEO Eiso Kant，主题是他所定义的模型工厂——即少量顶尖研究员加上工程化的训练流水线所构成的高频率出模型能力。团队最新交付的 Laguna S 是一款 118B 参数 MoE 模型，在开源权重下表现超过体量更大的 Thinky 约 1T 模型，验证了小团队通过更好的数据配方与训练调度也能追赶巨头。Eiso 分享了如何在有限算力下做数据管线、评测、代码专用后训练与 RLHF/RLVR，以及为何 AI 编码将是模型能力落地的第一战场。

洞察Laguna S 用不到十分之一体量赢 1T 稠密模型，说明训练配方 + MoE 工程化 + 代码专用后训练已经能显著抵消算力差距——这直接给中国代码大模型团队一条追赶路径，也让『招几十人做 1T 稠密』的公司显得笨重。评估候选人时应看是否掌握 MoE routing、data recipe 和 code post-training 三件套。
招聘行动把 Poolside 现有 30-50 名核心成员做深度 mapping(尤其 data / post-training / eval / infra)，作为 code-model 团队搭建的模版；同时对国内做代码模型的团队，把『MoE + 数据配方 + code post-training』写入招聘 must-have。
Latent Space · 2026-07-23 原文 ↗
★ 本周重点Frontier Radar社区/其他技术突破创始人/核心成员安全对齐多模态产品方向AI编码

Karpathy分享与大模型协作技巧：切到语音模式做十分钟意识流独白

Sometimes the LLM needs more bits to understand what you're trying to achieve, but you're too lazy to type them. Switch to voice mode and ramble for ~10 minutes.

Karpathy 在推文中分享自己与 LLM 协作的一个实用技巧：当觉得手动打字麻烦或需要传递更丰富上下文时，切到语音模式做约十分钟意识流式脑内独白，模型能从这种混乱松散的口语输入中重建出更清晰的结构化理解。他观察到模型对长段不连贯口述的重构能力很强，产出往往比自己动手整理还整洁，从而实现更好的思维对齐、减少后续来回修正。

洞察这条『语音独白 -> 结构化输出』的用法把 LLM 从『打字机』彻底变成了『思维协作面』，与 OpenAI 本周同期推 GPT-Live Voice、ChatGPT Work 的方向形成互相印证——未来产品分析师/PM/研究员岗位最重要的能力不再是 prompt engineering，而是『能否用嘴把上下文交代清楚』；这也意味着我们对候选人的表达/口述结构化能力评估权重要上升。
招聘行动在候选人评估流程中新增一轮『5 分钟脑内独白』环节，观察其能否用口语把复杂问题传递给模型并让模型高质量重构；重点识别有 voice-first agent 产品经验的产品/设计人才。
Andrej Karpathy 安德烈·卡帕西 · 2026-07-22 原文 ↗
★ 本周重点Frontier RadarAnthropic技术突破官方频道Scaling安全对齐产品方向

Anthropic 经济未来研究基金的研究议程

A research agenda for the Economic Futures Research Fund

Anthropic 公布 2 亿美元 Economic Futures Research Fund 的研究议程，围绕五大方向资助外部研究：企业内 AI 集成与劳动者话语权、AI 转型期的再培训与流动性、收入支持机制现代化（含无条件收入试点）、让劳动者共享 AI 增长红利（如资本账户或股权分红）、以及公共投资证据研究。单个项目资助规模 500 万至 3000 万美元，最低 100 万美元，面向高校、研究机构、政策组织与非营利机构申报；基金强调紧迫性，鼓励在里程碑节点即时共享阶段性发现。

洞察Anthropic 用真金白银把『AI 对就业的影响』从公关议题升级为长期研究基建，等同于给劳动经济学、产业组织、公共政策这类原本 AI 招聘忽略的学科打开了通道；其研究者未来大概率会反向进入模型 policy 团队，形成 AI × Econ 复合背景的稀缺人才池。
招聘行动在 mapping 系统里新增一条 AI × Economics/Policy 通道，锁定拿到该基金的 PI 与博后名单作为二年内候选池；同时提醒业务方，AI 招聘不再只看模型能力工程师，能量化『AI 对劳动力市场影响』的研究者正在成为战略资源。

为什么物理 AI 是下一个前沿——Applied Intuition

Why Physical AI Is the Next Frontier — Applied Intuition

The a16z Show 由 Marc Andreessen 与 Erik Torenberg 对话 Applied Intuition 联合创始人 Qasar Younis 与 Peter Ludwig，讨论物理 AI 崛起以及公司新发布的 Dana 平台——一款用于加速自主系统开发的新平台。Applied Intuition 十年来为乘用车、卡车、国防系统、矿业设备与工业机器人等提供智能机器软件；节目围绕自动驾驶、人形机器人、世界模型与仿真、AI 基础设施、真实世界安全部署的工程挑战、物理 AI 领域的全球竞争等话题展开，并阐述为何降低物理 AI 门槛将催生新一波产品与公司。

洞察从『软件世界 LLM』到『物理世界基础模型』的价值迁移正在被 a16z 系统性押注。Applied Intuition 的 Dana 与 Physical Intelligence（π0/π0.5）、Skild、Figure、1X 等构成新一波 Physical AI stack，谁能锁定 Sim2Real + Data Engine，谁就抢占下一代 Robotics Foundation Model。
招聘行动招聘/mapping 建议：加大对 Physical AI / Embodied AI 玩家的 mapping 力度——Applied Intuition、Physical Intelligence (π)、Skild AI、Figure、1X、Waabi 及 Waymo/Tesla FSD 老兵；重点识别华人在『仿真引擎 + Perception + Foundation Model for Robotics』方向的 Staff/Principal 级候选人。

★ 本周重点Frontier Radar社区/其他社区讨论技术社区/论文AI Agent

智能体集群与新模型经济学

Agent swarms and the new model economics
洞察这是 AI 编码工具从「单 Agent 辅助」进化到「多 Agent 协作」的关键拐点信号。Cursor 作为 AI coding 赛道营收领先者，其公开的 swarm 经济模型实际上在定义整个行业的下一代架构。这意味着 inference infrastructure（推理基础设施）团队的重要性将超过模型训练团队——需要能设计 agent 调度、成本优化、分布式推理的系统工程师。
招聘行动密切关注 Cursor/Anysphere 团队扩张（已融资数亿美元）；对标公司如 Windsurf、Augment 的 infra 团队招聘；具备分布式系统+LLM serving 经验的工程师是该赛道核心争抢人才。

★ 本周重点Frontier RadarOpenAI观点访谈深度长访谈推理开源生态安全对齐推理成本

Hugging Face CEO 谈开源 AI、模型路由与未来竞争格局

Hugging Face's CEO on Open Source AI, Model Routing, and the Future of Competition

Hugging Face 联合创始人兼 CEO Clément Delangue 做客 a16z，抛出的核心论断是——AI 的价值正在从单一大模型迁移到跨专用模型的工作负载路由层。他披露 Hugging Face 年化经常性收入已跨过 1 亿美元门槛，并讨论 GPT-5 之后的市场结构、开源模型对定价与监管的挤压、中国开源生态崛起对全球分工的冲击、以及本地化推理带来的隐私与成本红利。他还回应了关于开源安全风险的政策争议，强调透明与可复现是长期抵御滥用的正确路径。

洞察『模型路由 + 专用小模型』如果被证伪，未来两年最稀缺的不是超大规模预训练工程师，而是能设计路由策略、模型蒸馏与 SLM 工厂流水线的工程 leader；Hugging Face 一亿美元 ARR 也侧面证明中间件/平台层可以先于基础模型盈利，为投资并购 mapping 提供新坐标。
招聘行动把候选人档案里的『模型规模』标签细化成『pretraining / post-training / distillation / routing / serving』5 类，重点补齐 routing 与 SLM 蒸馏这两类目前中国团队普遍薄弱的能力；对 Hugging Face 内部路由/推理团队做穿透 mapping。

★ 本周重点Frontier Radar社区/其他观点访谈深度长访谈开源生态安全对齐产品方向

中国开源模型正把特朗普阵营的 AI 圈搅得内斗不休

China's AI models have Trump's AI world at war with itself

MIT Technology Review 记者 James O'Donnell 撰文分析：Moonshot 的 Kimi 等免费开源中国模型正在把特朗普任命的 AI 顾问阵营拉入公开互撕。文中援引 David Sacks 的表态，批评部分美国公司希望政府出手消灭开源竞争、以保住封闭模型的价差。文章梳理了开源浪潮对美国 AI 产业政策、出口管制、国家安全叙事的冲击，指出目前决策者正在被两股力量撕扯——保护封闭生态与利用开源摊薄成本——而中国模型迭代速度已经改变了博弈时钟。

洞察美国政策不确定性正在把中国开源模型团队(Moonshot / DeepSeek / Qwen 等)的核心研究员推到聚光灯下，短期会加剧他们被海外挖角的可能；但从长期看，也让中国团队的『开源 + 政策优势』成为吸引海外华人回国的差异化叙事，招聘节奏必须比历史上任何一次都更快。
招聘行动对 Moonshot、DeepSeek、Qwen、GLM 四大开源模型团队里所有署名论文一作/二作做即时高频雷达；同步准备中国团队针对海外华人的『开源 + 政策 + 算力自主』回国叙事包，服务战略招聘团队。

★ 本周重点Frontier RadarAnthropic社区讨论技术社区/论文AI编码

Claude Code现已使用Rust编写的Bun运行时

Claude Code uses Bun written in Rust now
洞察Anthropic 选择 Rust+Bun 技术栈揭示了 AI Agent 工具化的底层趋势：Agent 不再只是「调 API 的脚本」，而是需要毫秒级启动、极低内存占用的高性能系统软件。816 条评论的社区热度说明开发者极度关注 AI 工具的工程品质。这也印证了 Cursor 的 swarm 模型——多 agent 并行时，单 agent 的资源开销必须压到极致。
招聘行动Anthropic Claude Code 团队需要 Rust 系统工程师（非传统 ML 工程师）；AI coding 赛道正在分化为「模型能力层」和「工程基础设施层」两类人才需求，后者门槛高但竞争相对少，是候选人差异化切入点。

★ 本周重点Frontier RadarOpenAI观点访谈深度长访谈推理产品方向安全对齐AI Agent

认识 GPT-Red：OpenAI 打造的用于加固自家模型的 LLM 超级黑客

Meet GPT-Red: an LLM super-hacker OpenAI built to make its models safer

MIT Technology Review 高级 AI 编辑 Will Douglas Heaven 深度报道 OpenAI 自研的红队模型 GPT-Red——一个通过自博弈强化学习自动挖掘攻击方法、专门用来加固其他模型的攻防型 LLM。GPT-Red 发现了名为'伪造思维链'的新型提示注入攻击（在受害模型的推理笔记中植入虚假条目），其针对 GPT-5 的强攻击超过 90% 有效，但对最新 GPT-5.6 Sol 的成功率降至不到 23%，直接提示注入失败率低至 0.05%。OpenAI 在包含浏览器、邮件日历和代码编辑器的模拟真实部署环境中训练 GPT-Red，声明其仅作为人类红队补充且不会对外发布。

洞察「用 AI 攻击 AI」从概念走向工程化——GPT-Red 本质上是一个 adversarial RL agent，其训练范式（self-play + 环境模拟）与 AlphaGo 系列一脉相承。这暗示未来 AI 安全不再是手工编写规则，而是需要构建完整的「攻防对抗训练基础设施」。GPT-5 到 5.6 Sol 的防御提升（90%→23%）说明 scaling 对安全有正向效果，但攻击也在 co-evolve。
招聘行动关注 OpenAI Preparedness/Red Team 岗位及相关人才流动；具备对抗机器学习、渗透测试、RL 自博弈经验的研究者价值飙升。同时留意从 cybersecurity 领域转向 AI safety 的跨界人才。
MIT Technology Review · 2026-07-15 原文 ↗

用 12 个 subagent 做端到端 QA 的 prompt

"Do a full end-to-end QA test of OpenClaw with live API keys. Use 12 subagents to split up functionality, spin up dev ga

Peter Steinberger 展示一条 prompt：用真实 API key 对 OpenClaw 做完整端到端 QA，用 12 个 subagent 拆分功能、起不同端口的 dev gateway 做压测，自主编排、用 worktree 并自动开 PR，目标找出 200 个 bug，每次修复根因不打补丁，并持续更新桌面上的 markdown 测试报告。

英文原文 EN

"Do a full end-to-end QA test of OpenClaw with live API keys. Use 12 subagents to split up functionality, spin up dev gateways with different ports, and use some to stress test. Orchestrate, use worktrees and create PRs autonomously. Set a goal to find 200 bugs. Fix the root cause each time, no band-aids. Refactors are okay, unless they touch the plugin SDK boundary. Write a test report in my desktop folder as markdown and keep updating that."

洞察多 subagent 编排的实操 prompt，是 agent 工程能力的高阶范例。
招聘行动以此类 prompt 能力作为评估资深 agent 工程师的样本。

Madhu Guru 谈美国 AI 圈为何快速拥抱开放权重

In uncharted territory, answers to hard questions only come from repeated contact with reality. Been thinking about how

Meta AI 高级总监、曾在 Google 主导 Gemini/Veo/Nano Banana 的 Madhu Guru 反思，美国 AI 社区在不到一个月内就迅速形成对 open-weight 模型的支持，背后是 DeepSeek、微软与 OpenAI 分手、GLM、Kimi、Fable、OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 等一系列公开实验共同推动，各自揭示了激励、创新与地缘政治的不同侧面。

英文原文 EN

In uncharted territory, answers to hard questions only come from repeated contact with reality. Been thinking about how quickly the US AI community converged on support for open-weight models. It feels obvious now. It wasn't even a month ago. It took a series of public “experiments” to play out. DeepSeek. Microsoft-OpenAI breakup. GLM. Kimi. Fable. The OpenAI-Hugging Face episode. Each revealed something different about incentives, innovation, geopolitics, and the cards the different players were holding. We got a chance to jointly observe the first- and second-order effects of different directions and update our beliefs. I think that's how we'll figure out many of AI's biggest societal questions over the next decade.

洞察这是一位主导过 Gemini/Veo 的顶级模型人才，其立场变化反映行业风向，本人即 top-tier mapping 对象。

Rauch：用 agent CLI 加文件系统做研究

I started doing all my research with agent CLIs and the filesystem. A 📁 𝚛𝚎𝚜𝚎𝚊𝚛𝚌𝚑/ folder, an 𝙰𝙶𝙴𝙽𝚃𝚂.𝚖𝚍 describing the fo

Rauch 分享用 agent CLI 和文件系统做所有研究的工作流：一个 research 文件夹、一份描述偏好与最佳实践的 AGENTS.md，启动 agent 提问即可，可跨会话关联知识、无限扩展，还能让 agent 渲染 HTML 报告并部署到 Vercel，其软件本质就是 AGENTS.md 里的英文。

英文原文 EN

I started doing all my research with agent CLIs and the filesystem. A 📁 𝚛𝚎𝚜𝚎𝚊𝚛𝚌𝚑/ folder, an 𝙰𝙶𝙴𝙽𝚃𝚂.𝚖𝚍 describing the format I like and best practices. Then launch my agent and ask a question. No fancy apps, knowledge graphs, UIs. The agent can be asked to find and correlate knowledge from previous sessions. Scales to infinity. The folder can be synced via iCloud, git, etc. If I want to share some outcome, I can ask the agent to render a cool HTML report and deploy it to Vercel. The "software" that powers this is English in 𝙰𝙶𝙴𝙽𝚃𝚂.𝚖𝚍.

洞察极简、文件系统驱动的 agent 工作流是 AI-native 生产力样板。
招聘行动借鉴此工作流评估候选人的 AI-native 实操能力。

AI 是专家的放大器，专精更重要

The best way to think about AI is as a force multiplier for the fields you already know about (or for the rate at which

Aaron Levie 认为 AI 最好被理解为你已擅长领域(或想快速学习的新领域)的放大器；既无判断力又无意愿培养判断力的人只会产出 slop。专家会越来越强，因为他们能正确驾驭工具、在 agent 跑偏时纠正、把产出整合成有用的东西，因此专精在未来会更重要，市场期待也更高。

英文原文 EN

The best way to think about AI is as a force multiplier for the fields you already know about (or for the rate at which you want to learn about a new one). The 3rd category -no existing judgment, no interest in developing it- will basically produce slop and won’t drive that much economically productive activity. What will happen is that the experts will get better and better at their craft and be able to do so much more. Because they can wield these tools in ways that actually drive high quality output, veer agents in the right direction when they go off, and actually incorporate the work into something useful. The expert engineer with agents will do far more productive work, precisely because they know how to steer the agent properly. The designer will product far better outcomes using AI than someone without an eye for design. And so on. As a result of this dynamic, specialization will continue to be important, if not even more so as the tools get more powerful. Because the expectation of the market gets to be much higher. Getting good at any craft will continue to be necessary in the future.

洞察AI 时代对专精加会驾驭 agent 的复合人才需求上升，直接指导招聘画像。
招聘行动招聘中优先识别既有领域判断力又能高效驾驭 agent 的候选人。

This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. 反周期位置编码：Möbius 边界条件让上下文检索更可靠

Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable

论文提出 Möbius RoPE，一种基于反周期频率阶梯 θ_i=π(2i+1)/N 的旋转位置编码：每个旋转平面在训练上下文中前进 π 的奇数倍，位置和乐（holonomy）为 -1，序列首尾通过闭式 Dirichlet 偶极子确定性耦合。作者称这是首个反周期边界条件的位置编码，并预训练了 48 个 160M 与 410M 级别模型（每个 2B FineWeb-Edu tokens）进行验证。

英文原文 EN

Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. 上下文加权的离散流匹配

Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching

离散流匹配（Discrete Flow Matching）为离散结构生成建模提供了灵活框架，但标准分解式训练目标将条件良好的可预测 token 与模糊的高熵 token 混杂训练。作者发现 token 值的不确定性与其邻域上下文密度密切相关，为此对底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）进行简单修改，引入局部上下文信息，实现上下文加权训练。

英文原文 EN

However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information.

奖励通道中的暗室效应：稠密预测奖励为何摧毁 GRPO 训练的 LLM 智能体

The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works

针对稀疏奖励、长时程 LLM 智能体，稠密的逐步监督（如奖励其预测下一观测）看似能带动记忆能力提升。作者证明在 group-normalized RL（GRPO）下，该方案不仅无效，反而会摧毁策略。基于 ALFWorld 上的 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 实验，基于势能的预测奖励会使所有训练收敛到退化吸收态（预测精度趋近 1.0，任务成功率归零，回合被顶到时限），呈现由优化器自动构造的「暗室」病理。单因素消融进一步定位了成因。

英文原文 EN

Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design

确定性 KV-cache 淘汰按重要性分数保留 Top-k Token 并丢弃其余部分。作者证明该方案无法感知自身破坏了什么：被淘汰值可被人为更改，使推理系统所保留的一切保持不变，但真实注意力输出误差可以任意增长，因此没有任何服务端估计器能一致地评估该误差。随机化淘汰可恢复可识别性：以已知包含概率对尾部进行 Poisson 采样，一次 logit 偏移即可在 softmax 内完成 Hájek 校正，并可基于保留样本采用调查采样方差估计器。

Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy

Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion（SEDD）以无约束的正分数比率参数化离散逆过程。正性虽保证逆跳率非负，却不保证 Bayes 可实现性——含噪状态下的比率未必能由某个干净 Token 的后验在前向核下诱导得到。Score-entropy 损失的总体最优点正确，但偏离最优点时无法强制该约束。在训练完成的纯均匀 SEDD 检查点上，约四分之一的完整分数向量违反坐标盒约束，超半数虽落在盒内但仍存在偏差。

英文原文 EN

The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. 渐进式 Token Cramming：可靠的 Token 压缩及其揭示的边界

Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals

Token cramming 将序列压缩为可近乎完美重建的可学习嵌入，但固定预算与 99% 阈值让人无法分辨残余误差属于优化失败还是根本极限。作者提出渐进式 cramming，逐 token 扩展目标前缀，直到在固定优化预算内无法重建为止，发现其轨迹在嵌入空间中呈现低维结构，且将压缩嵌入拼接到输入前会在多项选择基准上带来温和但一致的准确率下降。

英文原文 EN

Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmark

Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design

实证流水线中高召回初筛决定了哪些样本进入后续复核，被漏掉的相关样本对下游而言彻底丢失。作者研究要以有限样本方式认证「漏检相关质量足够小」需要多少审计标签，并刻画了该问题的标签复杂度：仅使用候选集内标签无法给出任何非平凡界，审计必须对被排除池——唯一可能藏有未召回相关样本的区域——进行采样。

英文原文 EN

An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. 无限 agent 时代的安全与权限难题

Chatted with a friend who leads security at a public company following the GPT Sol incident. some interesting takeaways.

Madhu Guru 与一位上市公司安全负责人交流后指出，当一个员工能起数百个 agent、agent 还能再生 agent 时，为有限员工设计的身份与访问管理(IAM)面临根本挑战：agent 是否继承创建者权限，生命周期多长，子 agent 权限如何，又该如何审计。

英文原文 EN

Chatted with a friend who leads security at a public company following the GPT Sol incident. some interesting takeaways.. How do you manage security for effectively infinite agents when identity and access management was designed for a finite number of employees? One employee can now spin up hundreds of agents. Those agents can spawn more agents. Traditionally, each employee has an identity, a role, permissions, and a lifecycle. Do agents inherit the spawning employee’s permissions? What’s their lifecycle - a task, a ticket, a week? Do child agents inherit the same permissions? How do you audit all of this?

洞察agent 安全与权限治理是新兴刚需，催生新型安全人才需求。
招聘行动关注 agent identity 与安全治理方向的稀缺人才。

用中国训练的 LLM 不等于数据被拿走

Using a Chinese-trained LLM doesn’t mean they get your data. An LLM is basically a giant file full of numbers - that’s

Madhu Guru 科普，用中国训练的 LLM 不代表对方能拿到你的数据：LLM 本质是一大堆编码智能的数字文件，open-weights 模型可下载并在自己的云环境运行，模型训练方就此出局，数据留在你运行的地方。

英文原文 EN

Using a Chinese-trained LLM doesn’t mean they get your data. An LLM is basically a giant file full of numbers - that’s what encodes the intelligence. Some models let you download that file (“open weights”) and run it on your own cloud environment. The model trainer is out of the picture now. Your data stays wherever you’re running it.

AI 目前更像增强劳动而非替代

Very good post from the Head of Economics at Anthropic. They’re finding that jobs have been less negatively impacted by

Aaron Levie 引述 Anthropic 经济学负责人的观点，AI 对就业的负面冲击小于预期，因为 AI 目前仍需人来操作才能产生价值，多数工作只能自动化其中某些任务，自动化特定任务后反而能提高产出、维持甚至提升需求；原文称到目前为止 AI 既偏向技能又增强劳动，它补充领域专长、依赖 human-in-the-loop 引导和评估最复杂的工作，Levie 认为 Jevons 悖论依然成立。

英文原文 EN

Very good post from the Head of Economics at Anthropic. They’re finding that jobs have been less negatively impacted by AI than expected, as we continue to see time and time again in the data. The reason for this is that AI - at least so far - still requires people to operate to produce value in most cases. Most jobs can’t be fully automated with AI, only certain tasks in those jobs. And when you automate specific tasks, you actually can get even more output from those jobs, raising the demand (or at least maintaining it) in many cases. “So far, AI is both skill-biased and labor-augmenting. It complements domain expertise. It relies on humans in the loop to direct and evaluate the most complex work. And it rewards AI proficiency. Model capabilities are improving fast, but remain stubbornly jagged. To fill in the pockets of the jagged frontier, expert oversight is needed to steer incredibly capable AI systems, and to recover when they falter.” I suspect that we continue to see this in a number of critical areas of work. It’s clearly happening in software engineering, where software produced is being multiplied, all of which still requires developers to manage the work agents are doing. Software engineers are needed in a wide variety of industries now and companies of all sizes can now light up software projects that would have been impractical before. But there will be plenty of other domains of work where demand remains strong in a world where agents can accelerate the output of that job. Jevons paradox is alive and well.

洞察AI 增强而非替代专家的判断，直接支撑对高技能加 AI 复合人才的招聘逻辑。
招聘行动以 AI 增强专家的逻辑构建招聘画像，重点招高判断力人才。

Persian Pixel：面向波斯语的大规模合成OCR数据集

Persian Pixel: A large-scale synthetic OCR dataset for Persian language

尽管波斯语在多个国家由超过1.1亿人口使用，但其光学字符识别（OCR）的成熟度仍显著落后于拉丁语系语言。这一差距源于两大根本性挑战：波斯-阿拉伯书写系统的内在复杂性，以及大规模、高质量标注数据集的匮乏。波斯语文字具有强制性的连笔连接、上下文相关的字形塑形、丰富的连字、附加符号布局，以及在Naskh、Nastaliq等不同书写形式间的风格差异。

英文原文 EN

Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for Persian remains substantially less mature than for Latin-script languages despite Persian being spoken by more than 110 million people across multiple countries. This gap arises from two fundamental challenges: the intrinsic complexity of the Perso-Arabic writing system and the limited availability of large-scale, high-quality annotated datasets. 面向视觉-语言模型模态顺序一致性的测试时训练

Test-Time Training for Modality Order Consistency in Vision-Language Models

作者发现视觉-语言模型对一种语义无关的具体变化十分敏感：图像与问题呈现的顺序。跨三个模型与三个基准的实验一致表明，图像优先的提示方式持续优于问题优先的提示方式，揭示了一种可复现的模态顺序失效。作者利用这一差距设计了一种顺序一致的测试时训练方法，在所有评测设置下显著缩小了模态顺序差距。出人意料的是，该方法在更强的图像优先分支上也相对基线取得一致提升，从而形成自举增强效果。

A Collaborate Engine then sends the query and partial reasoning trace to a frozen LLM for completion through a single handoff. South Park Commons 合伙人、前 Dropbox CTO Aditya Agarwal 认为文化永远碾压战略；创业文化源自三处，创始人的性格与 DNA、早期团队的性格与 DNA、早期产品的性格与 DNA，三者相互交叠强化，既有自上而下也有自发涌现，创始人过弱或分歧过大都会伤害文化。

英文原文 EN

So much of conversation is on company Strategy…but Culture always eats strategy for breakfast. So what does Culture for startups mean? How does it get created? Where does it come from? Startup culture stems from 3 areas: —> The founder’s personality and DNA —> The early team’s personality and DNA —> The early products personality and DNA A lot of these overlap and reinforce each other. Some of it is top down….some of it is emergent. It sucks when the emergence is artificially dampened..because perhaps the founder is too weak to instittuionalize. It also sucks when there is so much difference of opinion that you end up getting pockets of different culture. I don’t want to get dragged into DEI nonsense. We can be from different backgrounds but all agree to be part of the same company culture.

Why Physical AI Is the Next Frontier | Applied Intuition

Causal Models Need Causal Data — Xaira's X-Cell model for Drug Discovery

RLM 论文揭示的评测作弊灰区

very notable trajectory comparison writeup here buried in the RLM paper from @a1zhang and @lateinteraction. an open sec

Swyx 点出 a1zhang 与 lateinteraction 的 RLM 论文中一段值得注意的轨迹对比写作，前沿模型训练的公开秘密是即便不直接在测试集上训练，也能通过训练测试相似样本来 goalseek 几乎任意基准分数；而开放权重发布时通常不给数据集与 RL 环境，因此有 plausible deniability，论文尝试用标准 NLP 距离度量分析隐藏轨迹，支持 RLM 能泛化到共享潜在结构的未见任务。

英文原文 EN

very notable trajectory comparison writeup here buried in the RLM paper from @a1zhang and @lateinteraction. an open secret of "frontier" model training is that even without training on test, you can basically cheat by training on test lookalikes, enabling you to goalseek almost any benchmark number you want. however when they are released open weights, 99% of the time the norm is that you do not get the datasets/rlenvs that would easily show you if someone was training on Temu Tbench, so there is plausible deniability. Alex and Omar discuss applying standard NLP distance metrics on hidden trajectories. There's no ultimate solution here, but they have some prelim explorations. It happens to support the finding that RLMs can generalize to unseen tasks that share latent structure observed in training.

洞察评测可信度与 RL 泛化是研究前沿，指向顶尖研究人才。
招聘行动关注 RLM 论文作者(a1zhang、Omar 等)作为研究人才。

多模型 agent 系统是未来：15 倍成本优化

Multi-model agentic systems clearly are the future. Great post by cursor. Their new research shows that a frontier model

Aaron Levie 引用 Cursor 的研究，用前沿模型做 planner 与 orchestrator、配合更便宜的 workhorse 模型，可显著降低整个项目的 token 成本，带来 15 倍成本改进；原文称大任务中真正需要前沿智能的时刻很少，如最初的分解、设计决策和某些权衡，一旦前沿 planner 把模糊性收敛成明确指令，便宜模型只需照做，Levie 认为按任务阶段路由到不同模型是复杂 agent 的核心设计模式，也是应用层在编码、金融、法律、医疗、生命科学等领域差异化的模板。

英文原文 EN

Multi-model agentic systems clearly are the future. Great post by cursor. Their new research shows that a frontier model as a planner and orchestrator with a workhorse cheaper model can materially lower the cost of total tokens on a project, resulting in a 15X cost improvement. “Few moments in a large task genuinely require frontier intelligence, such as the original decomposition, the design decisions, and certain trade-offs. Once a frontier planner has collapsed the ambiguity into a detailed, explicit instruction, less expensive models simply have to follow it.” This is increasingly becoming the core design pattern of complex agents, because the tokens used on large amounts of work on many tasks don’t require the same intelligence threshold as the planning step. By routing to different models based on where you are in the task, you get much greater efficiency overall. This provides the template for where the applied layer in AI will differentiate. You can only drive this efficiency if you know the domain well and have the ability to work with multiple model tiers; companies that can do this will across coding, finance, legal, healthcare, life sciences, and other critical spaces will be in a strong position to get larger workloads that would otherwise be too expensive for the customer to deploy.

洞察多模型路由加领域知识是应用层护城河，指向复合型应用工程人才。
招聘行动关注掌握多模型编排与垂直领域知识的应用层人才。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-07-21 ♥ 206 · ↻ 29 · 💬 37 原文 ↗

AI is more likely than humans to form biases when hiring

一个 agent 干活，另一个按 rubric 评审

Use one agent to do the work and another to review it against a rubric. @trq212 explains how this works for video short

Peter Yang 引用 Thariq 的做法，对这是不是好视频短片这种没有确定答案的问题，应让一个独立的验证 agent 按 rubric 评审并给反馈；因为模型偏好自己的输出(self-preferential bias)，验证自己时会更宽松。

英文原文 EN

Use one agent to do the work and another to review it against a rubric. @trq212 explains how this works for video shorts: “For something like, ‘Is this a good video short or not?’ you don’t have a deterministic answer. You should have a separate verification agent read a rubric, review the short, and give feedback. We call it self-preferential bias. When a model prefers its own output, it’s going to be more lenient at verifying it.” 📌 Watch the full episode here: https://t.co/mMrt4D5qU7

洞察生成加验证的双 agent 与自偏差，是 agent 质量控制的关键设计。
招聘行动面试中考察候选人对 evaluator 与 verifier agent 的理解。

英文原文 EN

We present the Bangladesh Legal Acts Dataset (BLAD), a curated collection of 1{,}484 legislative acts enacted between 1799 and 2025. Each act is represented with its full text, structured sections and footnotes, repeal status, and metadata linking it to the governing regime, head of state, and prevailing legal framework at the time of enactment. The corpus spans English, Bengali, and mixed-language documents, supporting temporal and multilingual analysis of statutory law. In this work we study the Best Policy Identification (BPI) problem in online, tabular Reinforcement Learning. This is an active sequential hypothesis testing problem in which the learner's objective is to identify an optimal policy in a Markov Decision Process (MDP) with high confidence, while minimizing the expected sample complexity to do so. We consider an online setting with deterministic rewards, where the agent must strategically navigate through the MDP in order to effectively explore. 网络安全是超级智能的最佳基准

Incidentally, I’m very bullish on cybersecurity as one of the best benchmarks for superintelligence. The “IQ test” of so

Rauch 非常看好把网络安全作为超级智能的最佳基准，即软件工程的 IQ 测试；他合作过的最好工程师往往有深厚安全背景；让模型一把梭克隆某产品很容易唬人但不是好测试，发现、修补、逆向、利用漏洞需要超越具体语言与框架的认知能力，真正考验推理力与角落思维，Kimi K3 在此表现出色，对开放模型是好兆头。

英文原文 EN

Incidentally, I’m very bullish on cybersecurity as one of the best benchmarks for superintelligence. The “IQ test” of software engineering. The best engineers I’ve worked with in my career have usually had a deep background or interest in security. It’s actually easy for a model to “one-shot an XYZ clone” and impress people on X. But that’s not a good test. Finding, patching, reversing, and exploiting require a cognitive skill that transcends programming languages, runtimes, frameworks… It demands true reasoning power from the model and “corner thinking”. Very, very few humans excel at this, let alone in ordinary day-to-day software writing. Seeing Kimi K3 do so well here bodes well for open models.

洞察以网络安全能力衡量模型与人才的推理深度，提供高区分度评估维度。
招聘行动招聘中把安全与逆向能力作为顶尖工程推理力的信号。
X / Guillermo Rauch · 2026-07-19 ♥ 82 · ↻ 8 · 💬 10 原文 ↗

强开源替代品面前，封锁反而更不安全

In a world where there are strong open source alternatives that are only just behind the frontier models, you make yours

Aaron Levie 认为，当存在仅略落后于前沿模型的强开源替代品时，封锁前沿模型能力反而让自己更不安全、更没竞争力；即便美国能完全禁用开源模型，其他生态没有此禁令、会占得先机，甚至反过来对付你，因此 AI 监管的算计必须正视开放权重远比预期更强这一事实。

英文原文 EN

In a world where there are strong open source alternatives that are only just behind the frontier models, you make yourself less secure and competitive by gatekeeping access to frontier model capabilities. If you play this out, even if America could fully ban access to open source models, other ecosystems wouldn't have that ban and they'd have a leg up on access to those models. They could use them against us -or just use them to better defend themselves- and our own companies wouldn't be able to keep up. The calculus of what you do about AI regulation simply must change to account for the fact that open weights models are far stronger than many expected.

洞察开放权重的地缘与安全逻辑，影响政策与人才生态。
招聘行动关注开放权重政策走向对人才流动的影响。

AI 变便宜，花费反而上升

A lot of people make the mistake of thinking that when AI costs drop, that spend on AI drops with it. Usually the opposi

Aaron Levie 指出很多人误以为 AI 成本下降、AI 支出就会下降，通常恰恰相反，AI 越便宜就被消费越多，因为你能负担把它用于更广的任务，写更多代码、审更多代码找 bug 与安全问题、在以前处理不了的大数据集上跑 agent；因此可预见的未来任何降低 token 成本的东西都会推高推理需求，这也解释了为何开源商业模式在 AI 里行得通，因为没人在设备上跑这些模型，都在 infra 上跑。

英文原文 EN

A lot of people make the mistake of thinking that when AI costs drop, that spend on AI drops with it. Usually the opposite happens. When you make AI cheaper, it will get consumed more. Because now you can afford to use for a wider of tasks than you did before. You start to write more code. You review more code for bugs and security. You run agents on large data sets you couldn’t process before. And so on. Thus, for the foreseeable future, anything that lowers the cost of tokens will drive up inference demand. This also gives you some insight also into why even open source business models work in AI. No one is running these models on their devices; they’re running them in infra. Great time to be one of those providers.

洞察Jevons 悖论支撑推理需求长期增长，指向 infra 与推理服务人才价值。
招聘行动关注推理 infra 与服务 provider 方向人才。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-07-19 ♥ 235 · ↻ 20 · 💬 40 原文 ↗
Follow Builders社区/其他Builder 推文Builder 声音

AI 扩散的速度受制于与现实的交互

Good post if you’re trying to understand AI diffusion. Progress driven by AI will ultimately be rate limited by its inte

Aaron Levie 推荐一篇理解 AI 扩散的好文，AI 驱动的进步最终受限于它与现实世界的交互速度；编码之所以采用快，是因为你能写近乎无限的代码、测试并运行、无需外部世界改变任何事，但生命科学(新药要多年测试)、销售(要与客户来回)、合同(要对方谈判)则不然；原文以 AI 给你一个绝妙的喷气涡轮叶片设计仍很可能失败、唯一办法是造出来试着弄坏它来说明现实反馈才是学习的真正上限，因此需要把智能真正嵌入现有行业工作流的应用 AI。

英文原文 EN

Good post if you’re trying to understand AI diffusion. Progress driven by AI will ultimately be rate limited by its interaction with the real world. The reason coding, for instance, has been adopted so quickly is you can write a near infinite amount of code, test it, and run it -and it can add value- without anyone in the outside world ever having to do anything differently. A single person, from a computer, can just make something work end-to-end differently. This is not true for life sciences, where new drug development eventually needs to be tested for years. Doing a sale, which requires going back and forth with a prospect. Or even a contract, which has to be negotiated on the other side by your counterparty. “An AI will hand you a genuinely clever design for a jet turbine blade. It might be far more likely to work than anything your engineers came up with. It'll still probably fail, because that's the base rate at the edge of what anyone knows. The only way to find out is to build the blade and try to break it. That's the real limit on learning, and it doesn't care how smart you are. Coming up with ideas was never the hard part. The hard part is how fast reality answers them.” Incidentally, this is why you need an applied AI that actually takes intelligence and makes it useful within the workflows of existing industries. Model outputs -alone- are not enough in most cases. You need to actually change the underlying workflows, and you need systems to deal with the realities of the real world feedback loops that are in these industries. This is why there’s so much opportunity in the applied AI layer.

洞察应用 AI 需改造真实工作流，指向懂行业加懂 AI 的复合人才价值。
招聘行动关注能改造垂直行业工作流的应用 AI 人才。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-07-19 ♥ 280 · ↻ 34 · 💬 32 原文 ↗

刚开始做内容该聊什么

Whenever someone asks me "I'm just getting started with posting content on social media; what should I talk about?" My

Zara Zhang 建议，想开始发内容却不知聊什么时，就想想朋友与同事最常问你的三个问题，把答案做成视频或帖子，用你现实中回答他们的方式说出来，那就是你的定位；重复说过三次以上的内容就值得做成视频。

英文原文 EN

Whenever someone asks me "I'm just getting started with posting content on social media; what should I talk about?" My answer is: What are the top 3 questions that you get asked most frequently by friends/acquaintances/colleagues? Turn those answers into videos/posts. Just say it like how you said it to them in real life. That is your positioning. If you have repeatedly said something for >3 times, it's worth turning into a video One of my initial motivations for posting on social media was because I was tired of repeating myself in real life

连续思维链(Continuous Chain-of-Thought)方法用短序列的密集潜在表示替代冗长的推理轨迹。本文提出C-MTP，一种更简洁、更快速的直接监督方法。

英文原文 EN

Continuous Chain-of-Thought methods replace verbose reasoning traces with a short sequence of dense latent representations. Earlier continuous CoT methods indirectly supervise the latent representations such that its final state match that of verbose reasoning traces, requiring autoregressive, slow generation during training. We introduce C-MTP, a simpler, faster direct supervision approach that models each latent as an average of the embeddings in the CoT traces to be compressed. 未来的实验室应像数据中心——Lila Sciences 的 Andy Beam 与 Rafa Gómez-Bombarelli

The Lab of the Future Should Feel Like a Data Center — Andy Beam & Rafa Gómez-Bombarelli, Lila Sciences
